Experiments: Wan-CoF在四个视频推理benchmark上相比Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基线有显著提升；通过注意力分析探究推理token在模型深度、去噪步数、空间和时间维度上的贡献。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。模型规模巨大（14B），不具备边缘/星上部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，Chain-of-Frame通过时间帧序列进行推理的思路，或许可启发遥感视频流中时序目标检测的推理机制设计，但与本课题的轻量化核心目标关联极弱。

Abstract: Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

</details>


### [67] [Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08765)
*Haoran Feng,Ruiyang Zhang,Longyi Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Canvas360是一个面向全景图像生成的两阶段框架，结合几何感知预训练与下游任务微调，并构建了100万对高质量全景数据集，用于风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑等上下文生成任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于全景图像生成（panoramic generation），属于计算机视觉中的图像生成/编辑领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：全景图像生成（panoramic generation），包括风格迁移、修复、外扩、编辑等上下文生成任务。应用场景：全景图像生成与编辑。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 两阶段框架（two-stage framework）：几何感知预训练 + 下游任务微调。关键模块包括并行深度生成（parallel depth generation）、速度循环填充（velocity circular padding）、相似性损失正则化（similarity loss regularization）。支持通过token级拼接实现统一上下文全景生成。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Canvas360Dataset，100万对高质量全景样本，用于风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑。评价指标：FAED（panorama-specific metric）及其他定量评估指标。

Experiments: 实验表明Canvas360在全景图像保真度上有所提升，在FAED指标上表现突出，在多项定量评估中达到有竞争力或领先的结果。摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比数据。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接关联，无可借鉴点。

Abstract: In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

</details>


### [68] [OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08766)
*Hongyu Liu,Chun Wang,Feng Gao,Xuanhua He,Yue Ma,Ziyu Wan,Yong Zhang,Xiaoming Wei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPSD-V，一种用于少步自回归视频扩散模型的后训练在线策略自蒸馏范式，通过引入真实长视频作为时序上下文提供密集轨迹级监督，减少长自回归推理中的误差累积和运动退化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视频扩散模型的自回归生成任务（video generation），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频生成（video generation），具体为少步自回归视频扩散模型的后训练优化。应用场景：长视频生成。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于自回归（AR）视频扩散模型，具体应用于Self-Forcing和LongLive两种代表性少步AR视频模型。核心机制：学生模型按推理时rollout生成每个chunk，教师模型在同一去噪状态下使用更干净的AR一致性时序缓存（真实视频替换旧历史）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化。论文使用知识蒸馏（self-distillation）范式，但目的是提升生成质量而非模型压缩或加速。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文关注生成质量而非推理效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体训练数据集。评价指标：VBenchLong分数、用户偏好研究（10名参与者，20个视频对，66.0%总体偏好，82.5%排除平局）。

Experiments: 在Self-Forcing和LongLive两种模型上应用OPSD-V，实验显示在视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong分数上有一致提升。用户研究显示OPSD-V优于基线模型。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 方法针对视频生成任务设计，难以直接迁移到遥感目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能值得注意的点是"on-policy self-distillation"的思想——在推理路径上进行自蒸馏，但该设计高度耦合于自回归视频生成的特殊结构（KV cache、chunk-wise rollout），难以直接应用于遥感目标检测模型。不推荐作为主要参考。

Abstract: We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).

</details>


### [69] [Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08769)
*Weijian Chen,Weibo Yao,Yuhang Zhang,Xiaolin Tang,Guo Wang,Weijun Zhang,Xitong Gao,Yihao Chen,Hongde Qin,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoLOG框架，用全景图像和几何-梯度分区策略实现大规模户外场景的3D高斯泼溅重建，并构建了首个大规模全景户外场景重建基准Pano360。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于3D场景重建（3D Gaussian Splatting）和全景图像渲染，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及大规模场景和计算效率，但核心任务是novel view synthesis/3D reconstruction，而非遥感目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大规模户外场景3D重建（3D Gaussian Splatting）；传感器类型：全景相机（equirectangular projection, ERP）；场景：户外大场景重建；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感相关内容。

Model architecture: 两阶段粗到精框架（coarse-to-fine）：全局粗阶段使用sky-sphere建模和全景单目深度监督；精化阶段使用G²PS（Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy）构建自适应包围体，通过视差驱动的不确定性和基于梯度的注意力评分分配相机。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到"block-parallel training"实现可扩展的块并行训练，但这是分布式训练策略，非模型轻量化。不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"scalable, block-parallel training"，但这是训练阶段的并行策略，不涉及推理阶段的星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 构建了Pano360数据集，是首个大规模全景户外场景重建基准。评价指标：渲染质量（rendering quality），具体指标摘要未说明。摘要未说明目标检测相关数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 实验表明G²PS在渲染质量上达到SOTA，并支持可扩展的块并行训练。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细对比数据、消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何遥感图像理解任务；2. 不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法；4. 不涉及星上部署或边缘推理。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点：其"block-parallel training"的分区策略思想，如果未来研究遥感大场景下的分布式在轨处理（如多星协同推理），或许有启发性，但关联非常间接。不建议投入时间深入阅读。

Abstract: Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08770)
*Cheng-De Fan,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Chen-Wei Chang,Chin-Yang Lin,Kun-Ru Wu,Yu-Chee Tseng,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongE2V方法，利用预训练视频扩散先验实现基于事件流的视频重建、预测和帧插值，通过自回归展开和自适应上下文切换解决长序列时间漂移问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于事件相机(event camera)的视频重建与插帧，属于神经形态视觉(neuromorphic vision)和视频生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：事件流视频重建、预测、帧插值；场景：通用事件相机视频处理；传感器：事件相机(event camera)；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频扩散模型(video diffusion priors)微调，引入Autoregressive Unrolling（自回归展开）、Adaptive Context Switching（自适应上下文切换）、Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction（重编码对齐与交叉残差校正）、Event Voxel Density Augmentation（事件体素密度增强）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。基于视频扩散模型的方法计算开销通常较大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 在真实世界基准数据集(real-world benchmarks)上实验，具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标涉及感知质量(perceptual quality)、时间一致性(temporal coherence)、零样本泛化(zero-shot generalization)能力。

Experiments: 在视频重建、预测、帧插值三个任务上均优于现有方法(state-of-the-art)，表现出优异的时间一致性和零样本泛化能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 基于扩散模型的方法计算量大，不满足轻量化需求；3. 未提供任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 仅针对事件相机数据，不适用于光学遥感图像、SAR图像等常见遥感数据模态。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。扩散模型的计算开销与在轨部署的轻量化需求相悖。不建议参考。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/

</details>


### [71] [ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08771)
*Fabio Tosi,Luca Bartolomei,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipDepth是一个仅6.1M参数的轻量级单目深度估计网络，通过重参数化编码器-解码器和大规模多域知识蒸馏，在零样本泛化与部署效率间取得最佳平衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于单目深度估计（monocular depth estimation），而非遥感目标检测；虽然涉及轻量化（6.1M参数、实时推理）和知识蒸馏，但任务类型完全不同，且未涉及遥感/卫星/在轨场景。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，仅轻量化方法（知识蒸馏+重参数化）有间接借鉴价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：单目深度估计（monocular depth estimation）；场景：通用视觉场景（多域训练集），非遥感/卫星场景；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：无（深度估计任务）。

Model architecture: 重参数化编码器-解码器（reparameterizable encoder-decoder），从基础模型（foundation model）进行大规模知识蒸馏。

Lightweight method: 知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）——从大基础模型蒸馏到小网络；重参数化（reparameterization）技术实现紧凑网络设计；模型参数量仅6.1M。

Onboard deployability: 参数量：6.1M；运行速度：实时（real-time rates），从服务器GPU到功耗受限设备均可运行；摘要未说明具体FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 5个基准数据集（five benchmarks）上进行零样本评估；摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在5个基准上达到轻量模型中最优的零样本精度与部署效率权衡；与参数量大50倍的基础模型相比，精度差距显著缩小。

Limitations: 1. 任务为深度估计，非目标检测，与遥感在轨检测方向无直接关联；2. 未涉及遥感场景（如卫星图像、SAR、多光谱等）；3. 未讨论在轨部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）相关适配；4. 未说明具体FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 知识蒸馏策略可借鉴：从大模型蒸馏到轻量网络，用于遥感目标检测模型的轻量化；2. 重参数化编码器-解码器设计思路可用于遥感检测骨干网络；3. 多域训练集策略可迁移到遥感多源数据（光学+SAR+多光谱）的域泛化检测；4. 但需注意任务差异（深度估计→目标检测），需重新设计检测头和损失函数。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

</details>


### [72] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Wat3R，一种跨域半监督学习框架，利用教师-学生架构和无标注水下视频实现从空气到水下场景的3D重建，无需水下标注数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦水下3D重建（depth estimation, point cloud reconstruction），不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Model architecture: 教师-学生架构（teacher-student architecture），跨域半监督学习框架，用于从空气域迁移到水下域的3D重建模型。

Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

</details>

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。论文本身未讨论实际硬件实现复杂度、实时性约束等。

Abstract: This paper presents a Multi-Map Dynamic-Entropy Intrusion-Aware Chaotic Modulation (MU-DE-IAEACM-MM) framework for adaptive physical-layer security in multi-user wireless systems. Unlike conventional chaos-based schemes that rely on static parameter secrecy, the proposed architecture treats entropy as a regulated security variable and dynamically updates chaotic control parameters using an entropy-driven adaptation law. Heterogeneous chaotic maps, including Logistic, Tent, Chebyshev, and Sine generators, are distributed across users to enlarge entropy dimensionality and reduce cross-user statistical dependence. A correlation-based intrusion metric is incorporated to detect improved adversarial reconstruction coherence and trigger controlled entropy escalation. Stability analysis establishes bounded convergence conditions for the adaptive update process. Monte Carlo simulations under AWGN, fading, impulsive, colored, and narrowband interference demonstrate a persistent BER gap between legitimate and mismatched receivers and measurable secrecy capacity gains over representative fixed-parameter chaotic modulation schemes. The framework maintains positive secrecy rates in dense deployments with up to 64 legitimate users and 30 passive eavesdroppers. The results indicate that entropy-regulated multi-map chaotic modulation provides a scalable and synchronisation-stable approach for adaptive physical-layer security in next-generation wireless and IoT networks.

</details>


### [74] [Optimal Multi-RIS Placement: Coverage-Guaranteed Sum Rate Maximization Under Inhomogeneous User Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07990)
*Abhishek Rajasekaran,Mehdi Karbalayghareh,Xiaoyan Ma,David J. Love,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种分层优化框架，通过贝叶斯优化和超图划分方法，在保证覆盖概率的前提下最小化可重构智能表面(RIS)的部署数量，并最大化系统期望和速率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中的RIS（可重构智能表面）空间部署优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 无线通信系统中的RIS部署优化，任务为最大化期望和速率并保证概率覆盖，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明任何深度学习模型或视觉模型结构。方法为基于可见性图的约束集合覆盖、交集超图的最小划分、以及贝叶斯优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为期望和速率(expected sum rate)和覆盖概率(coverage guarantee)。

Experiments: 提供了数值结果，表明所提框架能一致地识别出同时实现良好覆盖和吞吐量的RIS部署位置，且无不可实际的系统假设。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文研究的是无线通信中的RIS部署优化，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The realization of the full potential of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) in a wireless system is tied to their strategic spatial deployment. While existing literature primarily focuses on enabling fairness by maximizing coverage to navigate through obstacles, these approaches often fail to exploit the spatial distribution of user density to maximize throughput. Thus, to enable fairness without loss in throughput, we formulate a novel hierarchical problem that maximizes the expected sum rate of the system while guaranteeing probabilistic coverage with the least possible number of RISs deployed. To solve this multi-layered non-convex problem, firstly, we obtain optimal regions where we can deploy RISs to provide the coverage guarantee by solving a constrained set-cover problem on a visibility graph. Then, the minimum number of RISs we require to satisfy the coverage guarantee is obtained by a greedy minimum partitioning on an intersection hypergraph formed using the optimal regions. Finally, a Bayesian Optimization based approach is used to compute the final optimal RIS placement. Numerical results are provided to show that the proposed framework consistently identifies placements that jointly achieve good coverage and throughput, without impractical system assumptions.

</details>


### [75] [SpO$_2$ Predictor-Guided Stage-Wise Time-Frequency Reconstruction of Low-Quality Dual-Wavelength PPG for Oxygen Saturation Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07996)

Experiments: 实验表明所提方法在公开和私有数据集上均取得最低subject-level MAE。摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比、消融实验或统计显著性分析。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无可借鉴性；2) 摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等实际部署关键指标；3) 仅报告MAE，缺乏其他评价指标；4) 未讨论模型泛化性及跨设备/跨场景迁移能力。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。但可抽象出一个思路：**任务引导的联合优化**——类似地，在遥感目标检测轻量化中，可以用检测任务（如mAP）作为约束来引导剪枝/量化/蒸馏过程，而不仅仅追求模型体积或FLOPs的降低，从而在压缩的同时保持检测精度。不过这一思路在遥感领域已有相关工作。

Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SpO$_2$) estimation from wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for long-term health monitoring, but low-quality red and infrared PPG segments can distort waveform morphology and degrade SpO$_2$ prediction accuracy. Existing PPG denoising and reconstruction methods usually optimize waveform fidelity or heart rate characteristics, while time-domain waveform loss on PPG signals alone insufficiently preserves frequency structure and SpO$_2$-relevant information. This paper proposes a SpO$_2$ predictor-guided stage-wise time-frequency reconstruction framework for low-quality dual-wavelength PPG signals. The proposed method first selects high-quality PPG segments to pretrain a SpO$_2$ predictor. A masked reconstruction model is then trained to recover randomly masked PPG regions using a joint reconstruction objective that combines time-domain waveform loss with frequency-domain loss computed from the short-time Fourier transform (STFT). To make the reconstruction task physiologically relevant, the pretrained SpO$_2$ predictor is incorporated as an additional constraint, encouraging the reconstructed PPG to preserve SpO$_2$ information rather than only minimizing waveform reconstruction error. The SpO$_2$ predictor and PPG reconstructor model are optimized through four training stages. Experiments on the public OpenOximetry Repository and a private wearable PPG dataset show that the proposed approach achieves the lowest subject-level MAE, with 2.882\% on the public dataset and 2.359\% on the private dataset.

</details>


### [76] [Low-Rate Wrist SpO2 Estimation under Micro-Perturbations Using Motion-Aware Beat Selection and Perfusion-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08001)
*Zequan Liang,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Ehsan Kourkchi,Hossein Sayadi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级低采样率腕部血氧饱和度(SPO2)估计框架，通过运动感知心跳选择和灌注引导校准，在降低PPG传感器功耗的同时保持估计精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于可穿戴健康传感（wrist-based SPO2 estimation from PPG），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然论文标题包含"lightweight"且涉及功耗优化，但其轻量化对象是PPG传感器采样率，而非深度学习模型压缩，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，无需阅读。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，属于可穿戴健康传感领域，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：连续血氧饱和度(SPO2)估计；场景：可穿戴健康监测（腕部）；传感器：PPG（光电容积描记法）双波长信号 + 加速度计；目标：SPO2值回归估计。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法框架包含：beat-level AC/DC分量提取、beat-level R值计算、基于加速度计运动评分的加权策略、受试者特异性灌注参考校准。整体为信号处理+校准框架，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 轻量化体现在降低PPG传感器采样率（从100Hz降至25Hz）以减少传感器功耗，属于硬件/传感层面的功耗优化，而非模型层面的剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等深度学习模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟等部署指标。仅提及降低PPG传感器功耗（sensor power consumption）和25Hz低采样率实现能效优化（energy-efficient wearable implementation），与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：私有可穿戴数据集（private wearable dataset）；评价指标：MAE（2.305±1.113%）、RMSE（3.117±1.743%）。

Experiments: 在私有数据集上，25Hz采样率下达到最佳性能，MAE 2.305±1.113%，RMSE 3.117±1.743%，性能与100Hz采样率相当，同时降低了传感器功耗。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 使用私有数据集，无法复现；3. 轻量化仅涉及传感器采样率降低，不涉及模型压缩；4. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极为有限。唯一可能参考的思路是：在遥感在轨场景中，是否可以通过降低传感器采样率（如降低图像帧率或光谱波段采样率）来降低数据采集和传输功耗，但这与模型轻量化本身是不同层面的问题。

Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SPO2) monitoring from photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for wearable health sensing, but wrist-based SPO2 estimation remains challenging due to subtle wrist micro-perturbations and inter-subject differences in local perfusion status. These factors can destabilize the red-to-infrared ratio-of-ratios (R) and reduce the reliability of conventional fixed R-SPO2 mapping. This paper proposes a lightweight low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation framework that integrates motion-aware beat selection and perfusion-guided calibration. The proposed method extracts beat-level alternating-current/direct-current (AC/DC) components from dual-wavelength PPG signals, computes beat-level R values, and uses accelerometer-derived motion scores to weight beats within each sliding window. A subject-specific perfusion reference is further used to guide calibration across different perfusion conditions. Experiments on a private wearable dataset show that the proposed method achieves the best 25 Hz performance, with an MAE of 2.305$\pm$1.113% and an RMSE of 3.117$\pm$1.743%, while maintaining performance comparable to the 100 Hz sampling rate and reducing PPG sensor power consumption for energy-efficient wearable implementation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation under micro-perturbations.

</details>

*Zequan Liang,Sally Hang,Geneva M. Jost,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Hossein Sayadi,Ehsan Kourkchi,Setareh Rafatirad,Camelia E. Hostinar,Houman Homayoun*

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提及25Hz低采样率GSR特征与100Hz特征性能相当，暗示低采样率可降低计算开销，但未给出具体量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自采集的31名受试者配对腕部和手掌GSR数据，包含坐姿基线、站姿基线、中性说话、TSST四种任务场景。评价指标：平衡准确率（balanced accuracy），分别为0.823（TSST vs 坐姿基线）和0.871（TSST vs 站姿基线）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 样本量较小（31人），泛化性存疑；3. 仅使用随机森林这一传统机器学习方法，未涉及深度学习模型；4. 未提供任何模型复杂度、推理速度、资源消耗等部署相关指标。

Abstract: Galvanic skin response (GSR) is widely used for stress detection, but wrist-based GSR remains challenging because its absolute amplitude can differ substantially from laboratory-grade palmar measurements. In this paper, we propose a unit-independent low-rate wrist GSR processing pipeline to extract the number of skin conductance responses per minute (nSCR/min) as a stress-related feature. We collect paired wrist and palmar GSR recordings from 31 participants during sitting baseline, standing baseline, neutral speaking, and the Trier Social Stress Test (TSST), a laboratory social stressor task. The proposed pipeline cleans the raw GSR signal, decomposes it into tonic skin conductance level (SCL) and phasic skin conductance response (SCR), applies robust z-score normalization, and detects phasic SCR peaks to compute nSCR/min. Using random forest on 25Hz We-Be GSR, nSCR/min achieved balanced accuracies of 0.823 and 0.871 for binary classification between TSST and the sitting and standing baselines, respectively. Moreover, the 25Hz We-Be GSR features achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the original 100Hz features across the evaluated tasks. These results suggest the feasibility of low-rate, unit-independent wrist GSR processing for wearable stress detection.

</details>

Abstract: The dynamics of communication environments induce significant distribution shifts across domains, challenging the generalization of deep learning-based automatic modulation classification (AMC) models. While existing UDA methods alleviate this problem by aligning source and target features, they give limited consideration to modulation-specific structures that remain informative across domain conditions. In this paper, we consider signal prior knowledge, grounded in communication protocols and physical principles, as a potential way to enhance cross-domain representation learning. Given that different priors may vary in modulation discriminability, domain stability, and complementarity, this paper first analyzes five commonly adopted signal representations that instantiate different signal priors. From them, in-phase/quadrature (IQ), amplitude--phase (AP), and autocorrelation function (ACF) are selected as compact prior-guided inputs. Based on that, a dual knowledge and data-driven network (DKDNet) is proposed for cross-domain AMC. The multi-representation feature encoder (MRFE) and dynamic lightweight fusion unit (DLFU) are designed to achieve unified representation learning and adaptive feature fusion, and the resulting fused features are optimized with modulation classification and adversarial domain alignment objectives. Experiments on both simulated and public datasets validate the rationality of the prior selection and demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a pivotal technology for sixth-generation wireless networks to empower high-precision sensing. The demand for superior sensing resolution and the reality of spectrum fragmentation have driven the research of multi-band ISAC. Multi-band ISAC provides frequency diversity through independent observations across disparate bands, mitigating sensing performance fluctuations caused by frequency-selective radar cross-section compared to single-band counterparts. In this paper, we propose a framework for analytical performance characterization and resource optimization in multi-band ISAC systems. Specifically, analytical closed-form detection and false alarm probabilities for multi-band OFDM signals are derived, providing a theoretical foundation for subsequent resource allocation. Then, a joint power and time-frequency resource allocation scheme is developed and solved via a proposed ADMM-based algorithm to maximize detection performance. Numerical results validate the accuracy of the closed-form derivations and demonstrate the superior robustness of multi-band signals. Notably, the proposed optimization scheme achieves an 18 dB detection gain over traditional single-band baselines at a 90\% detection probability.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出一种低复杂度的截断Hankel Newton-MUSIC框架，用于可重构天线ISAC系统中的单快拍DoA估计，通过截断Hankel矩阵降低计算复杂度，实现线性复杂度的大阵列实时感知。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于ISAC（通感一体化）系统中的DoA估计问题，属于通信/雷达信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星对地观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及"低复杂度"和"实时性"，但方法本质是信号子空间分解与网格less角度估计，与遥感目标检测模型的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络等）无直接关联。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：DoA估计精度（接近方阵Hankel Newton-MUSIC）、运行时间（runtime reduction）。通过仿真实验验证。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明：所提方法在DoA估计精度上接近方阵Hankel Newton-MUSIC，同时大幅降低运行时间；对于大阵列，相比传统方阵Hankel MUSIC，运行时间降低两个数量级以上。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 方法针对ISAC系统的单快拍DoA估计，场景高度特定；3. 仅通过仿真验证，未在真实硬件或遥感数据上测试；4. 未涉及任何深度学习模型压缩或边缘部署技术。

Abstract: Reconfigurable antenna arrays can provide enhanced spatial Degrees of Freedom (DoFs) for Integrated Sensing And Communication (ISAC) systems, enabling high-resolution Direction of Arrival (DoA) estimation. In highly dynamic scenarios, however, DoA estimation must be performed within short coherence intervals, which often restricts processing to a single snapshot. Conventional subspace methods then suffer from rank deficiency, while Hankel-based spatial smoothing incurs high computational cost when the array size is large. This paper proposes a low-complexity gridless Truncated Hankel NewtonMUSIC framework for single-snapshot DoA estimation. The proposed method constructs a truncated Hankel matrix with a fixed row dimension to recover an effective signal subspace while reducing the cost of correlation construction and subspace decomposition. When the truncation length is independent of the array size, the dominant complexity scales linearly with the number of antenna ports. To reduce grid-induced quantization errors, coarse grid estimates are further refined by a secondorder Newton update in the continuous angular domain. Simulation results show that the proposed method achieves DoA estimation accuracy close to square Hankel Newton-MUSIC while substantially reducing runtime. For large arrays, it provides more than two orders of magnitude runtime reduction compared with conventional square Hankel MUSIC, making it suitable for realtime sensing in reconfigurable antenna-enabled ISAC systems.

</details>

Abstract: Atomic receivers, which leverage the quantum interference termed electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) for radio-frequency (RF) to optical signal transduction, offer a revolutionary paradigm for next-generation wireless communications. However, current information-theoretic characterizations are predominantly restricted to the Ξ-type of EIT path and rely heavily on the weak-probe approximation, which fails to predict the behavior of the atomic receivers under high signal-to-noise ratio regimes. In this paper, we establish a unified analytical model for atomic receivers, and apply this model to three typical quantum interference paths, i.e., V -type, Λ-type, and Ξ-type configurations. To provide a universal characterization, we propose the quantum coherence transfer coefficient (QCTC) to model the equivalent channel response induced by atomic receivers, using a steady-state perturbation framework built on the three-level EIT solution. The closed-form expressions of equivalent channel gains are then derived for three paths. Our results provide an analytical foundation for future capacity analysis and waveform optimization in atomic radio communication.

</details>

Abstract: Ultra-dense indoor next-generation networks suffer severe interference from mobility-induced blockages and localized multi-user hotspots that conventional digital twins~(DTs) cannot anticipate. We propose a generative AI~(GenAI)-enhanced DT framework employing a conditional generative adversarial network~(cGAN) with a spatio-temporal generator and PatchGAN discriminator for proactive rare-event channel synthesis. A worst-case zero-forcing~(WC-ZF) beamformer driven by Monte Carlo synthetic trajectories realizes distributionally robust precoding, with control-channel overhead bounded to $\approx$2.1\,kB per 10\,ms slot. Sionna-based simulations confirm a 5--8\,dB median signal-to-interference-plus-noise-ratio (SINR) gain, 60--70\% packet-loss reduction, and 60--85\% closure of the perfect channel state information (CSI) oracle gap within a 2.8--4.1\,ms inference overhead.

</details>

Abstract: Rydberg atomic receiver has emerged as promising candidate for next-generation wireless communication, due to the exceptional sensitivity and ability to overcome the physical limitations of traditional radio frequency antennas. Utilizing the resonant response of atomic energy levels for signal detection, Rydberg atomic receiver is inherently confined to a narrow instantaneous bandwidth. However, in high-mobility scenarios such as satellite communications, the severe Doppler effect induces carrier frequency offsets, which drive the signal beyond the instantaneous bandwidth and result in severe distortion. In this paper, we propose an adaptive local oscillator (LO) tracking Rydberg atomic receiver architecture designed to lock high-dynamic signals within the effective atomic response bandwidth. By employing a cross-product automatic frequency control (CPAFC) algorithm, the system dynamically estimates the instantaneous frequency offset, generates a corresponding error control signal, and adjusts the LO frequency through a feedback loop. Consequently, the intermediate frequency signal can always be locked close to the center of the atomic response bandwidth regardless of dynamics. Simulation results show that the proposed architecture significantly outperforms existing Rydberg atomic receiver, effectively alleviating performance degradation in high-dynamic environments.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB)-based performance bound analysis of cooperative multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) networks. We first show the CRLB transformation of the signal-level parameters to the state parameters (position and velocity) in cooperative ISAC networks. Unlike existing studies that primarily ignored coupling between position and velocity in the Fisher information matrix (FIM), we derive the full FIM and the corresponding exact CRLB. Particularly, the results of multi-monostatic sensing, multi-bistatic sensing, and their hybrid are discussed. Addressing the complexity and tractability, we simplify the FIM and CRLB by excluding the coupling terms between the position and velocity, and provide a criterion for determining whether the simplification is valid. The simplified CRLB benefits from low computational complexity and provides a tractable and reliable performance metric for optimization problems such as resource allocation and beamforming. Finally, the position and velocity CRLBs and the simplification-induced error are examined in the simulation. The results demonstrate that the simplified CRLB can be applied in general cases. Based on the simulation results, the impact of resource and geometric parameters on position and velocity error bounds, and the validity of the simplified CRLBs is explained through the corresponding CRLB expressions.

</details>

Abstract: In future 6G vehicular networks, users employing orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) waveforms may coexist under diverse mobility conditions, where both can experience high-mobility and low-mobility profiles. Since OFDM users can suffer severe inter-carrier interference (ICI) and OTFS users occupy larger spectrum resources, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) is a flexible framework that can efficiently handle these heterogeneous aspects. In this work, we propose a novel RSMA-assisted system to provide downlink communication to multiple OFDM and OTFS users. A common stream comprising the common messages of OFDM users spans the whole bandwidth to help OFDM users manage the ICI induced by potential high Doppler effects. OTFS users do not participate in the common stream. The private streams of OFDM users and the streams of OTFS users are transmitted over disjoint frequency bands. During the SIC process implemented at all receivers, channel estimation errors are taken into account. The simulation results highlight the impact of the power allocation factors and channel estimation errors on the system performance, and demonstrate the superiority of the proposed framework over orthogonal multiplexing in terms of outage probability and rate performance.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes an adaptive wavelet division multiplexing scheme for wireless systems serving users with heterogeneous mobility profiles over frequency-selective Rayleigh fading channels. By exploiting the multiresolution structure of the discrete wavelet transform (DWT), users are adaptively assigned to different decomposition levels according to their channel dynamics and Doppler conditions. A single-tap minimum mean square error (MMSE) equalizer is applied in the frequency domain, and the system performance is evaluated under realistic time-varying multipath fading environments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed adaptive allocation achieves balanced bit error rate (BER) across all user mobility classes while delivering substantial peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) reductions relative to both conventional orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and orthogonal time-frequency space (OTFS) modulation. The proposed framework is further validated in a four-user heterogeneous-mobility scenario, confirming its scalability and effectiveness to mixed-mobility multi-user scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: In 6G, MIMO dimensions continue to scale, yet the increased cost, power consumption, and hardware complexity associated with growing RF chains limit practical deployment. Parasitic antennas offer a promising alternative that can add spatial degrees of freedom and array gain without a proportional increase in RF chains. From a communication perspective, prior work on parasitic antennas has primarily focused on adjusting continuous reactance values using varactors, but such varactor-based tuning has increased cost and complexity in the analog control and practical RF circuit design. This paper proposes a multi-active multi-parasitic antenna (MAMP) architecture with binary controllers, where each parasitic element operates in one of two discrete reactance states. To validate the practicality of the system, we experimentally identify array geometries that best match the actual radiation patterns with those of the mathematical model through HFSS simulations. We express the induced current vector as a quadratic function of the binary state vector, and propose a pair of discrete reactance values that minimize the relative error of the proposed model while being implementable with off-the-shelf RF components. With these results, we develop two transmit beamforming codebook designs based on the generalized Lloyd algorithm. The first design exhaustively searches for all possible binary combinations to find the optimal solution, representing the theoretical upper limits of our framework. The second design leverages eigenvalue perturbation to significantly reduce computational complexity, making it suitable for online adaptation. Extensive simulations under various channel scenarios demonstrate that the proposed codebook designs enable MAMP with only few active antennas to achieve beamforming performance comparable to fully active antenna arrays with significantly more active antennas.

</details>

### [88] [Projected Energy Matching for Generative 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07749)
*Daniel Barco,Michal Balcerak,Suprosanna Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Philipp Denzel,Bjoern Menze,Frank-Peter Schilling*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出Projected Energy Matching框架，通过Helmholtz蒸馏和负缓存策略解决能量匹配模型在3D数据上的计算瓶颈，应用于医学CT稀疏视角重建。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模（能量匹配框架）；应用场景：医学CT稀疏视角重建；传感器类型：CT（计算机断层扫描）；目标类别：无（生成/重建任务）。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到"amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction"，但这是训练阶段的计算减少，非推理部署优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实医学CT数据（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：重建保真度（high-fidelity reconstructions）、伪影消除效果（resolving severe measurement artifacts）。

Experiments: 应用场景为医学CT稀疏视角重建，对比对象为standard energy matching。结果显示：计算量大幅降低（a small fraction），重建质量高，能消除严重测量伪影。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化部署完全无关；2. 仅针对生成式模型训练效率，不涉及推理加速；3. 仅在医学CT场景验证，未在遥感图像上测试；4. 未提供任何参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. Negative Caching策略作为一种内存优化思路，可迁移到遥感大模型训练中的显存管理；2. 如果未来在遥感生成/重建任务（如超分、去云）中需要训练能量模型，Helmholtz蒸馏可参考。但对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"这一核心课题，直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Energy Matching has emerged as a powerful generative framework that combines flow model efficiency with the explicit likelihood of Energy-Based Models (EBMs) via a single, time-independent scalar potential. However, directly training this potential on high-dimensional 3D data remains computationally challenging. While distilling a pre-trained flow model circumvents some of the initial training costs, we demonstrate that velocity fields inevitably contain non-conservative rotational artifacts (curl). Forcing a strictly conservative scalar potential to match this unconstrained field creates a "structural conflict", which degrades generation quality and mode coverage. To solve this, we propose Projected Energy Matching, a scalable framework that resolves these structural and computational bottlenecks. We introduce Helmholtz Distillation, a structural relaxation that leverages a Hutchinson trace estimator to explicitly absorb rotational noise into an auxiliary residual network. We subsequently refine this landscape using Negative Caching, a memory-efficient strategy that reuses negative samples across micro-batches, rendering sampling tractable during contrastive training with gradient accumulation. We deploy our method as an unconditional prior for real-world medical CT inverse problems, specifically sparse-view reconstruction. Ultimately, our amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction of that required by standard energy matching, while achieving high-fidelity reconstructions and successfully resolving severe measurement artifacts.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：鲁棒视频传输（robust video transmission）；场景：块擦除信道（block erasure channels）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出了一种神经视频压缩框架，包含空间域（spatial-domain）设计（视频帧分块、均匀擦除和语义引导的非均匀擦除策略）和特征域（feature-domain）设计（潜在特征分块、语义恢复缺失特征）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为重建质量（reconstruction quality），但未给出具体指标名称（如PSNR、SSIM等）。

Experiments: 在均匀和非均匀擦除概率下进行了实验，对比了空间域JSCC和特征域JSCC在不同擦除场景下的表现。

Limitations: 摘要未讨论局限性；与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，缺乏任何可迁移的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。其语义感知的鲁棒编码思想在极端情况下（如星地链路传输中的丢包恢复）可能有间接启发，但与本课题核心方向（检测模型轻量化、在轨推理）关联极弱。

Abstract: This paper investigates semantic-aware neural joint source-channel coding (JSCC) for robust video transmission over block erasure channels. We propose a neural video compression framework exploring both spatial-domain and feature-domain designs. In the spatial domain, video frames are partitioned into blocks, enabling localized erasure handling and fine-grained robustness control via uniform erasure and two-level, semantic-guided non-uniform erasure strategies. In the feature domain, latent features are partitioned, enabling missing features to be semantically recovered while maintaining overall spatial consistency. Comprehensive experiments quantify reconstruction quality under varying uniform and non-uniform erasure probabilities. Our results show that spatial-domain JSCC excels at handling random localized losses, whereas feature-domain JSCC provides superior robustness to distributed erasures and maintains fidelity under low-loss scenarios. The analysis highlights the trade-offs between spatial continuity and semantic redundancy, offering insights for designing robust, task-aware video communication systems.

</details>


### [90] [False Confidence: Automated Labels Confound Fairness Audits in Cervical Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07852)
*Linus Juni,Aasa Feragen,Aditya Parikh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文研究颈椎MRI分割中的公平性审计问题，核心发现是使用机器生成的"银标签"作为参考会高估模型性能并扭曲公平性结论，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是医学影像（颈椎MRI分割）的公平性审计，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像分割（cervical-spine MRI segmentation）；场景：临床工作流；传感器：MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：颈椎结构。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及"deployed model"用于分割，但未给出网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理或边缘部署方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CSpineSeg（颈椎MRI分割数据集）；评价指标：Dice系数（Dice points）；实验设置涉及性别、年龄、种族的公平性审计。

Experiments: 主要发现：1）部署模型在人口统计维度上是公平的；2）但参考标签（silver labels vs gold labels）的选择会显著影响性能评估和公平性结论——使用银标签比金标签高估约8个Dice点；3）银标签会导致公平性判断从"不显著"变为"显著"；4）提出了"false magnitude"（虚假幅度）和"false confidence"（虚假置信度）两个概念。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。从方法论角度看，论文讨论的"银标签偏差"问题在遥感领域标注中也存在（如自动标注的遥感目标检测数据集），但论文本身未涉及遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：论文提出的"参考标签来源影响评估结论"这一发现，对遥感目标检测模型评估有潜在警示意义——如果使用自动生成的伪标签（如从其他遥感模型迁移的标注）来评估轻量化模型性能，可能会高估模型真实能力。但论文本身未提供任何可直接复用于遥感轻量化目标检测的技术方法。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of cervical-spine MRI is increasingly used in clinical workflows, yet no fairness audit exists for this anatomy. We show that auditing these segmentation tasks is complicated by a common property of modern segmentation datasets: expert-annotated gold labels are expensive, so abundant machine-generated (silver) labels are added to limit annotation cost. This matters because the reference used to judge a model can itself be biased. In this study, we present the first fairness audit of cervical-spine MRI segmentation across sex, age, and race using the CSpineSeg dataset. We observe that the deployed model is demographically fair, but the choice of reference label, however, is not neutral. Because a dataset's silver labels are generated by a model trained on its gold labels, any new model trained on those same gold labels agrees more with the silver labels than with expert truth: scoring identical predictions against silver rather than gold overestimates performance by ~8 Dice points and turns the fairness verdict for age from non-significant to significant - not by the gap inflation Parikh et al. report (which we term false magnitude) but by collapsing within-group variance (which we term false confidence). Reference-label provenance is thus a first-order confounder in segmentation evaluation: performance and fairness should be reported against expert labels, and any fairness claim stated together with the provenance of its reference.

</details>


### [91] [SCI-Mamba: Unsupervised Learning based Low-Light Image Enhancement for Non-Cooperative Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08033)
*Yiyong Sun,Weihang Shan,Shijun Wei,Diwei Zhou,Guang Zhai*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SCI-Mamba，一种面向非合作航天器在轨服务的无监督低光照图像增强网络，结合自校准学习、VMamba架构和Retinex先验，并构建了Space Dark-1.0低光照航天器数据集。

Research relevance: 3分：论文涉及卫星/航天器在轨场景（on-orbit servicing, spaceborne imagery），且提到了轻量化（lightweight enhancement pipeline, resource-limited spaceborne hardware），但核心任务是低光照图像增强（low-light enhancement），而非目标检测。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向属于间接相关——低光照增强可作为检测的前处理模块，但论文本身不涉及目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 3分：论文与在轨场景相关且涉及轻量架构（VMamba），但核心任务非目标检测，可作为背景参考或前处理模块的候选方案，非核心必读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：低光照图像增强（low-light visual perception/enhancement）；场景：在轨服务（on-orbit servicing）、非合作航天器（non-cooperative spacecraft）、空间星载成像（spaceborne imagery）；传感器类型：可见光（orbital footage）；目标类别：航天器/航天器部件（spacecraft, components）。

Model architecture: SCI-Mamba框架：自校准无监督学习（self-calibrated unsupervised learning）+ VMamba架构（线性复杂度状态空间模型）+ Retinex物理先验（Retinex physical priors）。VMamba是Mamba（状态空间模型）的视觉变体，具有线性计算复杂度，相比Transformer的二次复杂度更轻量。

Lightweight method: 轻量网络设计：采用VMamba架构（线性复杂度O(n)而非Transformer的O(n²)），整体设计为轻量级增强流水线（lightweight enhancement pipeline），面向资源受限星载硬件（resource-limited spaceborne hardware）。摘要未明确提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等具体压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware"和"inference speed"优势，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化指标。与CNN、Transformer、Mamba方法的比较中强调了速度优势（inference speed），但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Space Dark-1.0（自建），包含真实轨道视频（real orbital footage）、暗室硬件在环测量（darkroom hardware-in-the-loop measurements）和物理约束合成数据（physically constrained synthetic data），覆盖多种光照、运动和姿态条件。评价指标：视觉真实性（visual authenticity）、色彩保真度（color fidelity）、推理速度（inference speed）。具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 与CNN-based、Transformer-based和Mamba-based方法进行全面比较，验证了SCI-Mamba在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度上的优势。消融实验和具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 核心任务是低光照增强而非目标检测，不涉及检测精度、mAP等指标；2. 未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化轻量级指标；3. 数据集Space Dark-1.0针对航天器场景，与遥感卫星对地观测场景（如舰船、飞机、车辆检测）差异较大；4. 无监督方法在极端低光照下的鲁棒性未充分讨论。

Ideas for my research: 1. VMamba架构（线性复杂度状态空间模型）可作为在轨目标检测backbone的轻量化候选，替代Transformer的二次复杂度；2. 自校准无监督学习策略可借鉴用于在轨场景中缺乏标注数据时的模型自适应；3. Retinex物理先验与深度学习结合的思路可用于遥感图像去雾/去噪等预处理；4. Space Dark-1.0数据集的构建方法（真实+半实物仿真+物理约束合成）可借鉴用于构建在轨目标检测训练数据；5. 低光照增强可作为在轨目标检测的前处理模块，提升暗光条件下的检测性能。

Abstract: Low-light visual perception acts as the core visual foundation for on-orbit servicing missions targeting non-cooperative spacecraft, supporting autonomous rendezvous, pose estimation, component detection and robotic capture operations. Spaceborne imagery suffers from severe low-light degradation, while the extreme scarcity of paired normal/low-light space samples severely limits the generalization capacity of supervised enhancement algorithms. To address this practical bottleneck, this paper proposes SCI-Mamba, an unsupervised enhancement network for low-light orbital spacecraft observations. The proposed framework unites self-calibrated unsupervised learning, linear-complexity VMamba architecture and Retinex physical priors, delivering a lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware. We construct Space Dark-1.0, a dedicated low-light spacecraft dataset integrating real orbital footage, darkroom hardware-in-the-loop measurements and physically constrained synthetic data covering diverse illumination, motion and attitude conditions. Comprehensive comparisons with CNN-, Transformer- and prevailing Mamba-based approaches verify the advantages of SCI-Mamba in visual authenticity, color fidelity and inference speed. The proposed framework provides a practical low-light enhancement solution for close-proximity non-cooperative space operations.
  The code is available at https://github.com/bitswh/SCI-Mamba

</details>


### [92] [ConRad: Efficient Conformal Prediction for Radiomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08084)
*Matt Y. Cheung,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ConRad框架，利用共形预测为医学影像分割衍生的影像组学特征构建自适应预测区间，提升区间效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于医学影像（medical imaging）中的影像组学（radiomics）和共形预测（conformal prediction），与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：共形预测框架用于影像组学特征的不确定性量化；场景：临床医学影像；传感器类型：2D医学影像（CT/MRI等）；目标类别：影像组学特征（radiomic targets）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。核心是ConRad框架，利用预测掩膜、输入图像、预测影像组学、边界不确定性作为协变量构建自适应预测区间。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：5个2D医学影像数据集；指标：区间效率（interval efficiency）、经验覆盖率（empirical coverage）；保留171个影像组学目标。

Experiments: 与基线方法对比，ConRad在保持近名义覆盖率的同时提升了特征级区间效率；消融实验表明分割边界不确定性特征是区间效率的最大贡献者。

Limitations: 仅针对医学影像场景，未涉及遥感图像；未讨论计算效率或部署可行性；共形预测框架的计算开销未评估。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但共形预测作为一种不确定性量化方法，理论上可迁移至遥感分割/检测模型的不确定性评估，不过与本课题"轻量化"核心方向不直接相关。

Abstract: Radiomic features derived from medical images and segmentation masks are used to support decision making in clinical imaging pipelines. In practice, these features are often computed from predicted masks, but segmentation models can be overconfident or poorly calibrated, making derived measurements appear more reliable than they are. Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but black-box intervals for segmentation-derived radiomics can be inefficient because they ignore test-time information about image appearance, mask geometry, and segmentation uncertainty. We propose ConRad, a conformal framework for scalar radiomic targets that uses covariates derived from the predicted mask, input image, predicted radiomics, and boundary uncertainty to construct adaptive intervals while maintaining coverage. Across five 2D medical imaging datasets and 171 retained radiomic targets, we show that ConRad improves feature-level efficiency compared to baselines while maintaining near-nominal empirical coverage. Ablation results further indicate that segmentation boundary uncertainty features are the largest contributors to interval efficiency.

</details>


### [93] [Partial-Reference IQA Based on Hermite-Gauss Structural Prediction and Texture Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08563)
*Paolo Giannitrapani,Elio D. Di Claudio,Giovanni Jacovitti*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种部分参考图像质量评估框架PreSPA，通过结构感知和纹理敏感两个互补指标分解感知质量，仅需从参考图像中提取一个标量先验μ，在多个基准上媲美甚至超越无参考方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于图像质量评估(IQA)，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化模型部署中的任何核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像质量评估(IQA)；场景：通用图像质量评价；传感器类型：通用自然图像；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明遥感或卫星相关场景。

Model architecture: PreSPA框架包含两个互补指标：1) 结构感知指标(无参考方式)，通过Hermite-Gauss预测畸变梯度场及其曲率角方差来捕获结构退化；2) 纹理敏感指标，通过参考与畸变复梯度图在强边缘区域的能量差及弱结构区域累积得到的标量先验μ估计局部噪声。最终分数通过仅含三个可解释参数的仿射融合得到。

Lightweight method: 方法本身具有轻量特性：仅需从参考图像提取一个标量μ，参考足迹降至信息论最小值；仿射融合仅含三个可解释参数，无需数据集特定校准。但这不是面向模型压缩/加速的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是算法本身的紧凑设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"computationally efficient"（计算高效），但缺乏量化指标。未涉及星上/边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速。

Datasets and metrics: 在6个标准IQA基准数据集上评估，摘要未列出具体数据集名称。评价指标为与Full-Reference和No-Reference方法的对比精度。摘要未说明具体指标名称（如PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS等）。

Experiments: 在6个标准基准上，PreSPA持续媲美或超越领先的无参考方法，在某些情况下达到全参考模型的精度。摘要未提供具体数值对比、消融实验或速度对比。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2) 缺乏参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等量化指标；3) 仅针对通用图像质量评估，未在遥感图像上验证；4) 未讨论在轨部署可行性；5) 未涉及目标检测任务中的质量评估应用。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) 其"仅需一个标量先验"的极简参考信息设计思路，可类比于轻量化模型中"最小化额外开销"的设计哲学；2) 三个可解释参数的仿射融合思路，可用于设计轻量级后处理模块。但整体与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低。

Abstract: We propose PreSPA (Partial-Reference Structural Prediction Approach), a Partial-Reference Image Quality Assessment framework that decomposes perceptual quality into two complementary indices. A structure-aware index, operating in a No-Reference manner, captures structural degradation through Hermite-Gauss prediction of the distorted gradient field and the angular variance of its curvature. A texture-sensitive index estimates local noise through a scalar prior $μ$, obtained from energy differences between reference and distorted complex gradient maps on strong-edge regions and accumulated over weakly-structured ones, reflecting the perceptual leakage of degraded edges into surrounding textures. Crucially, $μ$ is the only information extracted from the reference and is computed once per image pair, reducing the reference footprint to its information-theoretic minimum. The final score is produced by an affine fusion with only three interpretable parameters, making the method compact, transparent, and computationally efficient, with the viewing distance embedded into the operator scale and no dataset-specific calibration. Extensive evaluations on six standard benchmarks show that PreSPA consistently rivals or exceeds leading No-Reference approaches, while in several cases matching the accuracy of Full-Reference models.

</details>
