Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是评估效率（减少计算成本），而非模型本身的轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: Open VLM Leaderboard（用于验证评估框架），评价指标涉及统计显著性、置信区间宽度（CI width）、计算成本降低比例。

Experiments: 在Open VLM Leaderboard上验证，在2.5-point CI width allowance条件下实现80%的计算成本降低，同时保持统计显著性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。论文讨论的是模型评估的统计方法论，而非模型本身的设计或压缩。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。该论文的序贯检验思想在理论上可迁移到轻量化模型评估场景（如动态决定需要多少测试样本才能可靠评估轻量化模型的精度），但与本课题核心方向（在轨目标检测模型轻量化）无直接可借鉴的技术点。

Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

</details>


### [197] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，将工业系统异常检测重构为基于外生变量的Granger因果一致性验证，通过多尺度对齐和梯度矩阵监控因果关系的断裂来实现异常检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于工业系统时间序列异常检测，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"anomaly detection"一词在遥感中有应用（如变化检测），但本文方法基于时间序列因果推断，与遥感视觉目标检测任务范式完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对因果推断在异常检测中的理论方法有特别兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：工业系统时间序列异常检测；场景：复杂工业系统运维；传感器类型：时间序列传感器（非遥感光学/SAR/高光谱）；目标类别：异常/故障模式（非舰船/飞机/车辆等遥感目标）。

Model architecture: CAAD框架：基于Granger因果一致性验证，包含外生变量残差建模、多尺度对齐模块、梯度矩阵因果断裂监控模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向工业系统离线分析，非星上/边缘实时推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 真实工业数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标为异常检测精度（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在真实工业数据集上，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于多数SOTA基线方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/视觉领域；2. 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论；3. 方法基于时间序列因果推断，难以直接迁移到遥感图像目标检测任务；4. 未报告计算开销指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) 因果推断思想——在遥感目标检测中，可探索因果推理增强模型对遮挡、小目标等场景的鲁棒性；2) 多尺度对齐策略——可借鉴到遥感多尺度特征融合中；3) 异常检测中的"因果偏差量化"思路，或可用于遥感图像中的变化检测任务。但这些均为间接启发，非直接可复现方向。

Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [198] [Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08561)
*Dan Yamins,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从NeuroAI角度理论证明：对于足够困难的任务，任意两个最小DNN解的网络表征会呈现"弱对齐"保证"强对齐"，且对齐会沿网络层级"拉链式"传播，导致特权轴从端到端任务优化中涌现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是NeuroAI（神经科学与人工智能交叉）领域的理论工作，讨论DNN与大脑网络的表征对齐与趋同进化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文是纯理论分析，未涉及具体任务场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。仅提及"minimal DNN solutions"（最小DNN解）作为理论分析对象，未给出网络设计细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为理论推导，未使用任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未报告实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 论文是纯理论工作，未涉及任何工程应用或实验验证。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。论文讨论的是DNN与大脑网络的表征对齐理论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来研究轻量化模型与原始大模型之间的表征对齐（如知识蒸馏中的表征匹配），本文关于"弱对齐保证强对齐"的理论或许有参考价值，但需要大量额外工作才能转化到遥感检测场景。

Abstract: A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.

</details>


### [199] [Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08581)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Ngoc Anh Khong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从谱分析角度研究极限学习机(ELM)中基于伪逆计算的数值稳定性问题，探讨奇异值分解与迭代方法在不同条件下的可靠性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于ELM的数值稳定性理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务场景。

Model architecture: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM)，基于Moore-Penrose伪逆计算输出权重。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 合成矩阵数据及ELM基准测试集。评价指标为奇异值、条件数等数值稳定性度量，而非检测精度或推理效率指标。

Experiments: 在合成矩阵和ELM基准上对比SVD伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法，结果表明SVD方法在病态条件下更可靠，迭代方法对谱特性更敏感。

Limitations: 论文为纯理论数值分析，未涉及任何实际遥感应用场景或目标检测任务，也未讨论模型压缩或部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。ELM本身是一种快速训练方法，但其数值稳定性分析对遥感在轨场景下的模型鲁棒性有一定间接启发意义，但缺乏直接可借鉴的轻量化或检测技术。

Abstract: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.

</details>


### [200] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，通过贝叶斯准则提升决策在对抗扰动下的稳定性，而非仅追求名义最优性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文核心是**实验设计（experimental design）**和**主动学习（active learning）**中的决策鲁棒性问题，属于贝叶斯决策理论和统计学习范畴，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、神经网络模型压缩或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：对抗鲁棒决策感知实验设计（adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design）；应用场景：科学实验设计（scientific experiments）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法基于贝叶斯决策理论（Bayesian decision theory）构建实验设计准则，未涉及神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或任何边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成数据集（synthetic）和真实科学数据集（real-world scientific datasets）；评价指标：决策稳定性（decision stability）和可靠性（reliability），而非检测精度或模型效率指标。

Experiments: 实验表明：传统决策感知设计（conventional decision-aware design）可能快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策（fragile decisions），而所提鲁棒性感知方法（robustness-aware approach）在对抗扰动下能产生更稳定可靠的决策。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度、部署可行性等关键信息，也未涉及任何视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：在遥感在轨目标检测中，如果考虑对抗扰动（如传感器噪声、光照变化、大气干扰）对检测决策的影响，可以借鉴其"决策稳定性"的思想来设计鲁棒的轻量化检测模型，但论文本身未提供可直接复现的技术细节。

Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [201] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于跨异构队列的心血管疾病风险预测，在保护隐私的同时提升了预测性能。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于医学健康领域的**心血管疾病风险预测**，使用**联邦学习**进行隐私保护下的多中心协作建模。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感相关主题；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等轻量化相关技术。联邦学习本身是一种分布式训练范式，与模型轻量化/在轨部署无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：心血管疾病风险预测（生存分析/深度学习分类）；应用场景：医学健康、跨机构隐私保护协作；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提及"deep survival models"（深度生存模型）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Lifelines（148,230名参与者，自报告结局）和Rotterdam Study（10,155名参与者，数字化链接临床结局）；评价指标：C-statistic（一致性统计量）。

Experiments: 联邦学习训练的深度生存模型优于本地单独训练的模型。Rotterdam Study的C-statistic从0.728提升至0.739；Lifelines的C-statistic从0.783提升至0.787。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关。联邦学习作为一种分布式训练范式，理论上可用于遥感多星协同训练场景（多颗卫星各自持有本地数据，在不共享原始数据的情况下联合训练检测模型），但本文未涉及遥感场景、目标检测或轻量化方法，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [202] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于偏依赖的解释引导学习方法，通过约束模型对特定特征的响应来使其符合先验领域知识，提升回归任务性能和可解释性。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于模型可解释性（interpretability）和解释引导学习（explanation-guided learning），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归任务（regression problems），包括动力系统预测（dynamical systems forecasting）。不涉及遥感场景、目标检测、分类任务。传感器类型、目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构，仅提及"neural networks"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提及在多个回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上进行了实验。

Experiments: 实验表明：使用所提方法训练的模型比无约束模型表现更好、数据效率更高，且解释结果与用户提供的先验知识一致。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对回归任务验证，未涉及分类或目标检测任务；2. 需要用户提供先验知识（functional domain knowledge），这在遥感在轨场景中可能难以获取；3. 未讨论计算开销和实时性；4. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可抽象思考：如果未来在遥感在轨场景中需要让模型输出符合某些物理先验（如目标尺寸约束、几何不变性），类似解释引导的思路或许可用于约束轻量化模型的输出行为，但与本课题核心方向距离较远。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [203] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无码本的二元球面编码框架BiSCo-LLM，用于大语言模型的极端低位权重重压缩，通过球面二值化编码+残差BSQ+逐类别恢复蒸馏实现高效压缩。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的权重重压缩，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或遥感图像理解。虽然量化/压缩方法本身属于轻量化技术范畴，但应用场景(LLM推理部署)和对象(语言模型权重)与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。除非对球面编码这一特定量化技术有理论兴趣，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM权重的极端低位压缩；应用场景：大语言模型部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: BiSCo-LLM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面编码；(2) 残差BSQ阶段编码重建误差；(3) 逐类别恢复蒸馏。辅助8-bit保护通道用于敏感通道的稳定化。

Lightweight method: 极端低位权重重压缩方法：二元球面编码(Binary Spherical Coding, BSQ)，无码本设计，残差编码，逐类别恢复蒸馏，8-bit保护通道。属于模型压缩中的量化方法，但面向LLM权重而非检测模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及存储预算包括二元编码、神经解码器、保护通道负载、LoRA适配器和元数据，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 方法针对LLM权重结构设计，不一定适用于CNN或检测模型；(3) 摘要未给出任何量化性能指标(如perplexity、压缩比、推理速度等)；(4) 未讨论实际硬件部署效果。

Ideas for my research: (1) 球面编码的思想：将权重映射到超球面进行二值化编码的思路，理论上可迁移到检测模型的权重量化中，但需要验证对检测精度的影响；(2) 残差编码+蒸馏的混合压缩策略值得关注，类似思路可用于遥感检测模型的渐进式压缩；(3) 保护通道机制：对敏感通道单独用更高精度保护，这一思路可用于遥感小目标检测中关键特征通道的保护。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为LLM和检测模型的结构差异大。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [204] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种跨多MDP环境的层次化机器教学算法，用于学习对环境动力学鲁棒的奖励函数，解决逆强化学习中奖励泛化性差的问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是逆强化学习(IRL)中的奖励函数泛化问题，属于机器人/自主智能体的奖励学习范畴，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：逆强化学习(IRL)中的奖励函数学习与泛化；应用场景：自主智能体(autonomous agents)的多环境部署；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出了一种层次化机器教学算法(hierarchical machine teaching algorithm)，包含两个阶段：(1) 贪心选择信息量大的环境以暴露互补的奖励约束；(2) 在这些环境中策略性地查询低成本的反馈。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：regret（遗憾值）和held-out environments上的泛化性能。

Experiments: 实验对比了uniform teaching baselines，在相同反馈预算下，所提方法实现了更低的regret和更强的泛化能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要本身未讨论遥感、目标检测、轻量化等任何相关技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习/逆强化学习理论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [205] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有向无环图（DAG）共识的去中心化联邦学习框架gspDAG-FL，利用gossip传播历史实现模型更新的安全最终性，无需中心服务器或区块链协调。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文聚焦去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的共识机制与拜占庭容错问题，属于分布式机器学习/区块链交叉领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：去中心化联邦学习中的安全共识与拜占庭容错。应用场景：分布式节点协作训练，无中心服务器。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测模型架构。提出的是联邦学习框架gspDAG-FL，包含gossip协议、事件证书收集、拓扑DAG构建、Hashgraph风格虚拟投票、紧凑全节点证书等共识机制组件。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化方法。论文讨论的是通信/协调开销的降低（减少协调瓶颈、提高吞吐量），而非模型本身的剪枝、量化、蒸馏或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。实验在MNIST和Penn Treebank上进行，网络规模N=100，与星上部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST（图像分类）、Penn Treebank（语言建模）。评价指标：学习质量（接近基于验证的账本FL）、吞吐量、无效来源检测率。未涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标（mAP、F1等）。

Experiments: 在N=100节点规模下，gspDAG-FL在混合拜占庭和懒惰参与者场景下，学习质量接近基于验证的账本FL，同时减少了协调瓶颈、提高了吞吐量并保持了高无效来源检测率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何遥感场景验证。2. 实验仅在MNIST和Penn Treebank小规模任务上验证，未在目标检测或大模型上测试。3. 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署。4. 摘要未说明通信开销的具体数值或与基线方法的定量对比。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，其去中心化gossip通信思想或可启发星群卫星间的分布式模型更新协作，但论文本身未涉及遥感场景或模型轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>


### [206] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EdgeRefine，一种基于局部差分隐私的图神经网络边级隐私保护框架，通过Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并自适应加噪，在隐私-效用权衡上优于现有方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦图神经网络的边级差分隐私保护，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然GNN可用于遥感场景理解，但本文核心问题是隐私保护，与在轨遥感目标检测及轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图节点分类、图分类；应用场景：隐私保护图学习；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 基于GNN（GAT、GCN等）的图学习框架，核心模块为EdgeRefine的边级差分隐私机制，包括Jaccard相似度估计、边概率排序、基于隐私预算ε的边采样控制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ACM、Cora、AMAP（图节点分类/图分类数据集，非遥感数据集）；评价指标：节点分类准确率、图分类准确率、相对绝对误差（对抗图重构攻击）。

Experiments: 在ε=2.5下，ACM数据集上GAT准确率提升17.8%，Cora数据集上GCN准确率提升19.7%；图分类相比无噪声基线平均准确率下降约5%；图重构攻击下相对绝对误差均值在Cora上为1.962，AMAP上为1.472。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率、模型大小等部署指标；3. 仅在学术图数据集上验证，未在遥感场景下测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接联系是：若在轨遥感目标检测涉及图结构数据（如场景图理解），其隐私保护方法可参考，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化、实时推理、星上部署）无直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [207] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预算感知的测试时模型选择策略（Resample-or-Reroute, RoR），在LLM服务中动态分配预算于重采样和重路由之间以最大化正确性，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的服务路由与预算分配问题，属于NLP/LLM serving领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM测试时模型选择（test-time model selection）；应用场景：LLM服务部署中的成本-质量权衡；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测或遥感模型架构。核心是路由策略（routing policy）设计，而非网络结构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文讨论的是服务层面的预算分配（budget-aware allocation），而非模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。论文面向云端LLM服务，非边缘/星上场景。

Datasets and metrics: 使用四个不同难度的benchmark上的多模型正确性张量（multi-draw correctness tensors）进行回放实验；评价指标为成本-质量Pareto前沿（cost-quality Pareto front）。

Experiments: 与single-route、one-commit-router、budget-aware best-of-K、cascade、random-allocation等基线对比，RoR策略在Pareto前沿上表现更优，在最具异质性的benchmark上增益最大；消融实验表明增益受verifier质量门控。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、压缩方法或部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然"预算感知的资源分配"这一思路在概念层面（如星上计算资源有限时如何在多个检测模型或推理策略间分配预算）可能有极抽象的启发，但论文本身不提供任何遥感目标检测或模型轻量化的具体技术，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [208] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无训练松弛式推测解码（relaxed speculative decoding）技术，旨在加速大语言模型自回归采样，通过统一框架对比多种松弛方法并给出实践建议。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的推理加速，属于自然语言处理/生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等经典技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM自回归采样加速（speculative decoding）；应用场景：大语言模型推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 采用辅助草稿模型（drafter）+ 主LLM验证的推测解码架构，主模型和草稿模型均为自回归语言模型。

Lightweight method: 涉及推测解码（speculative decoding）中的松弛策略（relaxation），但并非传统意义上的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。论文强调松弛方法不适合轻量级专用多token预测草稿器（lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是LLM推理加速，而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法，仅提及在当代设置下进行基准测试并给出实践发现。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。松弛方法依赖草稿模型本身是好的语言模型，不适用于轻量级专用草稿器。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。推测解码中的"草稿-验证"并行加速思想（用轻量模型生成候选，主模型并行验证）在概念上可类比于遥感目标检测中的级联检测或粗检-精检策略，但论文本身不涉及遥感或目标检测，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [209] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中的流动性放置问题，使用图强化学习选择边添加以最大化s-t最大流，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是比特币闪电网络（Bitcoin Lightning Network）中的流动性放置问题，属于区块链/加密货币网络领域。完全不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、地球观测（Earth observation）、航空图像（aerial image）、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任何视觉检测任务；虽然文中提到"lightweight agent"，但这里的轻量是指图神经网络策略网络的轻量，与模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）或边缘/星上部署无关。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：比特币闪电网络中的流动性放置（liquidity placement），即给定预算选择通道打开以最大化路由容量。场景：区块链支付网络。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 轻量级agent，结合消息传递策略网络（message-passing policy network）与PPO（Proximal Policy Optimization）和动作掩码（action masking）。

Lightweight method: 文中"lightweight agent"指策略网络参数量小，但并非遥感领域关注的模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs等具体指标。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文部署场景是生产环境中的节点推荐（peer recommendations），执行4640次通道打开决策，分配267.3 BTC。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）、边缘部署、实时推理等完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实闪电网络快照（real Lightning Network snapshots）。评价指标：s-t最大流（max-flow）。

Experiments: 在真实闪电网络快照上，方法在多个种子和未见过的图上持续优于强启发式基线。已部署到生产环境。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。论文研究的是图上的组合优化问题，与遥感图像目标检测和模型轻量化属于完全不同的领域。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [210] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"超级权重"（Super Weights）概念在LLM剪枝中的普适性，发现单独训练超级权重会导致性能崩溃，而结构化低秩更新（如LoRA）才是有效的微调方式。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是LLM（大语言模型），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或轻量化部署等任何相关主题。虽然涉及剪枝（pruning）和参数重要性分析，但讨论的是语言模型的权重特性，而非遥感目标检测模型的轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对剪枝理论基础有特别兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM剪枝与微调方法分析；应用场景：大语言模型（OLMo-1B, OLMo-7B）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B Transformer架构，关注down_proj层和attention权重矩阵。

Lightweight method: 涉及剪枝（pruning）中的"超级权重"概念分析，但核心结论是否定该方法的有效性。同时涉及LoRA低秩微调方法。不涉及量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息，如参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提到随机猜测水平（random-guessing levels）作为性能对比基准。

Experiments: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B上实验：单独训练超级权重（100到8192参数）性能降至随机猜测水平；扩展到局部邻域（最多36K参数）无改善；随机选择相同数量的参数训练反而有提升；Vanilla LoRA仅用0.16%参数成功；10次随机种子消融实验验证结果统计无差异。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM，结论不直接适用于视觉模型或遥感目标检测模型；2. 摘要未说明实验数据集和具体评价指标；3. 未讨论该方法在视觉任务上的可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可借鉴以下思路：1. 论文揭示了"参数重要性不等于参数可训练性"这一发现，在遥感目标检测模型剪枝时，不能仅依赖参数重要性评分（如magnitude-based pruning），需考虑结构化剪枝策略；2. LoRA的低秩结构化更新思路可迁移到遥感模型的参数高效微调（PEFT），但需验证在视觉骨干网络上的有效性；3. 对"超级权重"概念的质疑提醒我们在遥感模型轻量化中应谨慎对待单一指标驱动的剪枝方法。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


### [211] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用UMAP内部构建的kNN图（而非其低维嵌入）进行数据分析，通过PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等图算法增强数据理解，并在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上验证有效性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于纯数据可视化/流形学习领域的通用方法研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为高维数据探索与理解（data sensemaking），场景为通用机器学习，数据集为MNIST和Fashion MNIST（手写数字/服饰图像分类），非遥感场景。

Model architecture: 核心结构为UMAP内部构建的kNN图（k-nearest-neighbor graph），在此基础上应用PageRank、k-core decomposition、clustering coefficient等标准图算法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST、Fashion MNIST。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体量化指标，提及定性（qualitative）和定量（quantitative）评估，与k-medoids、HDBSCAN等方法对比。

Experiments: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上验证了PageRank（代表性数据点选取）、k-core分解（密集核心vs稀疏外围）、聚类系数（紧密邻域检测）的有效性，并与k-medoids、HDBSCAN等专用方法对比，显示竞争力或互补性。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 未讨论计算效率或模型部署问题；3. 仅在MNIST/Fashion MNIST两个小规模数据集上验证，缺乏大规模遥感数据实验；4. 未与遥感领域常用方法对比。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可注意：UMAP的kNN图思想若迁移到遥感特征空间，可能用于遥感数据分布分析或异常检测，但与本课题的"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"核心方向关联极弱。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [212] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLORR，一种无状态、保持架构的在线低秩正则化方法，通过Hoyer稀疏度与核范数的GPU友好近似，在不修改模型结构的前提下提升神经网络的可压缩性。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文聚焦于通用视觉模型（ImageNet）和LLM的低秩正则化压缩方法，不涉及遥感/卫星场景，不涉及目标检测任务，也不涉及在轨部署。但其低秩正则化方法可迁移至遥感目标检测模型的轻量化场景，有一定借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 3分 — 方法本身有创新性（无状态、保持架构的低秩正则化），对遥感目标检测模型轻量化有潜在迁移价值，但论文完全不涉及遥感场景和目标检测任务，且缺乏部署相关指标，优先级中等。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像分类（ImageNet-1K）和语言模型预训练；场景：通用视觉与NLP；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。

Model architecture: ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, ViT-L/16, ResNet-18（视觉）；135M和560M规模的LLM（语言）。SLORR本身不修改模型架构，直接在原始权重矩阵上施加正则化。

Lightweight method: 低秩分解（low-rank factorization）压缩方法。核心创新是SLORR：一种训练时低秩正则化框架，基于Hoyer稀疏度度量（Hoyer sparsity metric）和核范数（nuclear norm），使用GPU友好的前向/后向近似计算，无需SVD分解、无需额外可训练参数、无需状态缓存。训练后可通过SVD对权重进行低秩分解实现模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及训练开销：视觉模型<8%训练额外开销，LLM<1%平均训练额外开销。未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageNet-1K（视觉）；未说明具体LLM预训练数据集。评价指标：压缩后模型性能保持程度（preserve performance），未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 实验1：在ImageNet-1K上对ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16进行短周期继续训练（short-horizon continued training），以及对ResNet-18进行预训练（pretraining），SLORR诱导可压缩性，训练开销<8%。实验2：在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR-Hoyer训练的压缩模型性能保持显著优于未正则化模型，训练开销<1%。

Limitations: 1. 仅在分类任务和语言模型上验证，未在目标检测、遥感场景下评估；2. 未报告压缩后的实际模型大小、推理速度、FLOPs等关键轻量化指标；3. 未与剪枝、量化、蒸馏等主流轻量化方法对比；4. 未讨论在资源受限边缘设备上的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 1. SLORR的低秩正则化方法可迁移至遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列、RetinaNet等）的训练过程中，提升模型的可低秩分解性，从而在训练后通过SVD压缩模型；2. Hoyer稀疏度度量与核范数的GPU友好近似计算方式，避免了传统低秩正则化中SVD的高计算开销，适合遥感大模型训练场景；3. 可尝试将SLORR与知识蒸馏或剪枝结合，形成"正则化+压缩"的复合轻量化流程；4. 需在遥感目标检测数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）上验证SLORR对检测精度和模型压缩率的trade-off效果。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Context Graph的主动式企业AI框架，通过Delta Detection Engine和Proactivity Scorer实现无需用户查询即可主动推送信息，在合同管理、工程响应、销售管道三个企业场景验证效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦企业AI中的主动式信息推送（proactive agents），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：企业级主动信息推送（proactive information surfacing）；应用场景：合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道管理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Context Graph（实时关系数据结构）+ Delta Detection Engine（状态变化监测）+ Proactivity Scorer（紧迫性/相关性/角色匹配评分）+ LLM驱动的Surfacing Layer（排名通知与解释生成）。使用NetworkX和Anthropic Claude API实现。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计或边缘部署相关技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗、实时推理或星上/边缘部署相关内容。仅提及"mean time to surface"从47分钟降至30秒以下，但这属于企业通知延迟而非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个企业场景的案例研究（contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, sales pipeline hygiene），非公开遥感数据集。评价指标：Precision@5（0.83）、false positive rate（0.11）、mean time to surface（从47分钟降至30秒以下）。

Experiments: 在三个企业场景中评估，Precision@5达到0.83，假阳性率0.11，主动推送时间从47分钟（反应式基线）降至30秒以下。摘要未提供与遥感目标检测方法的对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何视觉感知或目标检测任务；3. 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 仅在通用企业场景验证，无遥感数据实验；5. 依赖闭源LLM API（Claude），不适合星上部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的是"Delta Detection Engine"中持续监测状态变化的思想，在遥感时序变化检测（change detection）场景中可能有概念层面的启发，但论文本身未涉及遥感或视觉任务，且无轻量化设计，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [216] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署中的任何一个核心要素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [217] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [218] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [219] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Infinity-Parser2，一个用于端到端文档解析的大规模多模态模型，通过可控数据合成流水线+多任务强化学习解决标注数据稀缺问题，并发布两个变体（Flash低延迟版和Pro高精度版）。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于文档解析（document parsing），包括版面分析、表格/公式/图表解析、文档VQA等，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测（object detection）在遥感图像上的应用。虽然模型有低延迟变体（Flash），但轻量化设计是针对文档解析场景的推理加速，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化需求无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对多模态大模型的数据合成或多任务RL训练技术有跨领域兴趣，否则优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：文档解析（document parsing）、版面分析、表格/公式/图表/化学式解析、文档VQA。应用场景：文档数字化、OCR后处理。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：文档元素（bounding boxes, Markdown/HTML/LaTeX/SMILES等格式内容）。

Model architecture: 大语言多模态模型（Large Multimodal Model），共享架构的两个变体：Infinity-Parser2-Flash（低延迟优化）和Infinity-Parser2-Pro（高精度优化）。具体网络结构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到Infinity-Parser2-Flash通过某种方式实现了3.68×吞吐量提升（相对于Infinity-Parser-7B），但具体轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）摘要未说明。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到Flash变体有3.68×吞吐量增益，但未给出绝对延迟或推理速度数值。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建Infinity-Doc2-5M（500万样本，中英双语，涵盖多种文档类型）。评价指标：olmOCR-Bench（87.6%）、ParseBench（74.3%）。对比方法：DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5、MinerU2.5。

Experiments: Infinity-Parser2-Pro在olmOCR-Bench上达到87.6%，在ParseBench上达到74.3%，超越对比方法。Flash变体吞吐量提升3.68×。消融实验、详细对比结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 轻量化方法细节未披露，仅给出吞吐量增益倍数。3. 模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟等关键效率指标未报告。4. 仅针对文档解析场景，领域迁移性未知。

Ideas for my research: 1. 数据合成流水线（controllable rendering + iterative refinement）的思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测数据增强或合成，例如合成不同视角/光照/分辨率的遥感图像。2. 多任务联合强化学习（multi-task RL）的设计思想可用于遥感多任务学习（如检测+分割+场景分类联合优化）。3. Flash变体的低延迟优化思路（具体方法需进一步查阅原文）可能对星上推理有启发。但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点有限。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [220] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于Google开源LLM的向量化教学辅助模型VectorizationLLM，用于MATLAB课程教学，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [221] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [222] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI（特别是LLM、RAG、多智能体系统）在精算承保流程中的应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是精算科学（actuarial science）中的承保决策自动化，涉及LLM、RAG、多智能体系统。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化模型或星上部署等任何相关主题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为精算承保（underwriting）中的直通式决策（straight-through decision），场景为小型商业企业主保单（Business Owner Policies, BOPs）。传感器类型、目标类别均不适用。

Model architecture: 涉及三种管线：(i) 单LLM基线, (ii) 朴素RAG系统, (iii) 多智能体"Agentic RAG"管线（结合定向检索、第三方数据检查、显式多步规则评估）。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成（synthetic）但真实的实验环境。评价指标未在摘要中明确列出。

Experiments: 比较三种承保管线，Agentic系统在需要多步推理和缺失信息场景下表现最佳。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、压缩方法或部署策略。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。论文讨论的是精算领域的LLM/RAG应用，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [223] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向强化学习对齐的反馈操作正则化方法（FMR），用于改进模仿学习策略的对齐能力，与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究强化学习中的对齐问题（RL alignment），属于机器学习/强化学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。与本研究方向基本无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Experiments: 论文在Safety Gymnasium环境中验证FMR方法，展示了最高98%的对齐错误减少，且在有限数据场景下保持鲁棒性。但无任何与遥感目标检测相关的实验。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [224] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个关于尼日利亚工业机械使用与故障的小型数据集，并提出了基于链式推理(CoT)的数值推理方法，用于提升语言模型在工业场景下的领域接地性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何模型压缩或部署技术。论文聚焦于非洲工业机械数据集的构建和语言模型的数值推理能力提升，属于NLP/数据工程方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：工业机械数据集的构建与语言模型数值推理；应用场景：尼日利亚制造业和油气行业的机械使用与故障分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及使用语言模型构建数据集和链式推理(CoT)示例，但未给出模型名称或结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset，包含89条机器级记录，覆盖28个指标，时间跨度2006-2025年；另有94条prompt-completion-reasoning trace数据行。评价指标：领域接地性（domain-grounded prompts比例从1/78提升至94/94）、检索答案匹配率（84/84）。

Experiments: 主要实验：对比了早期发布版本（78条prompt中仅1条领域接地）与当前版本（94条全部领域接地）；检索答案全部匹配源数值（84/84）。未涉及模型检测性能对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 作者明确承认局限性：仅有89条记录，其中17个指标只有单次观测值，属于参考性和种子数据集而非大规模训练集；大多数推理行为是检索而非多步计算。与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 对本课题无可借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/数据工程方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [225] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型(LLM)中的人格特质(persona)分解、测量与控制，使用OCEAN人格框架和低秩适配器(LoRA)来放大或抑制特定人格特质，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究对象是大语言模型(LLM)的人格特质(persona)建模与控制，涉及OCEAN人格框架、LoRA适配器、LLM-judge评估等，完全不涉及遥感(remote sensing)、卫星(satellite)、目标检测(object detection)、轻量化(lightweight)或边缘部署(edge deployment)等本方向核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。论文任务是大语言模型行为人格(persona)的分解、测量与控制，应用场景为LLM安全与行为对齐，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 使用低秩适配器(LoRA, Low-Rank Adapters)对LLM进行微调以放大或抑制特定人格特质。涉及6个模型（4B-32B参数规模），来自3个模型家族。不涉及任何视觉检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 论文使用了LoRA（低秩适配）方法，这本身是一种参数高效的微调方法，但论文目的不是模型压缩或加速，而是人格特质控制。不涉及剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。不涉及参数量(除模型规模4B-32B外)、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用特质特异性多项选择基准(trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks)、标准能力评估(standard capability evaluations)、LLM-judge（经人类验证面板校准）进行评估。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 在6个模型（4B-32B，3个模型家族）上实验，发现：每个适配器对目标特质的移动大致单调且随规模变化；多个适配器可近似加性组合以构建混合人格；在中等规模下保持能力基准性能；沿神经质(neuroticism)和宜人性(agreeableness)轴移动分别影响挫败感(frustration)和谄媚(sycophancy)行为。还引入无监督心理测量管线，从模型输出中恢复4个可解释行为因子（语调、主动性、说教性、认知谨慎）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。论文本身未讨论遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一间接关联：LoRA作为一种参数高效微调方法，在遥感目标检测模型微调（如基础模型适配特定遥感场景）时可能有参考价值，但论文并未涉及视觉任务或遥感场景。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [226] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于视频的自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)患者自我刺激行为的自动检测，使用LSTM/GRU和I3D模型在SSBD数据集上进行行为分类，探讨了时序采样率和数据增强策略。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文虽涉及"remote behavioral screening"（远程行为筛查），但这里的"remote"指远程医疗/远程诊断，而非遥感(remote sensing)。论文处理的是人体姿态视频行为分类，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等概念完全无关。不涉及目标检测任务，也不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频行为分类（self-stimulatory behavior detection）；应用场景：自闭症临床辅助诊断；传感器类型：RGB视频摄像头；目标类别：自我刺激行为（非遥感目标）。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆/小目标/旋转框检测）完全无关。

Model architecture: LSTM、GRU、I3D（Inflated 3D ConvNet）。均为时序行为识别模型，与遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列、Faster R-CNN、RetinaNet等）无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化或部署技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上部署或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset；评价指标：accuracy（准确率）、mean loss（平均损失）；实验设置：不同帧采样间隔（1, 5, 15, 30, 45, 90帧）、10种数据增强策略消融、个性化机器学习（per-subject temporal split）。

Experiments: LSTM峰值准确率97.5%，GRU峰值准确率98.75%（采样间隔15帧时），均超过此前CNN基线（62-76%）。数据增强消融：水平翻转最高独立准确率48.78%，上采样(upsampling)缺失导致最大性能下降。个性化模型平均损失1.84，标准差0.79。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的遥感相关方法或结论；2. 数据集规模小（SSBD），且为临床行为视频，非遥感图像/视频；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 未讨论实时推理或资源受限部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是：时序采样率对视频模型性能的影响分析思路（不同帧间隔的实验设计），但遥感视频/时序目标检测场景与行为分类差异极大，且本文未涉及轻量化，参考价值极低。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames. For the second objective, ten data augmentation strategies were applied to an I3D transfer learning pipeline, with an ablation study quantifying the marginal contribution of each technique. Horizontal flip achieved the highest standalone accuracy (48.78%), while exclusion of upsampling from the augmentation pipeline produced the largest performance degradation, indicating its necessity for complex behavioral video augmentation. A personalized machine learning approach, in which per-subject models were trained and tested on temporally split segments of each video, produced consistent predictions (mean loss 1.84, SD 0.79). These results provide practitioners with concrete guidance on architecture selection, sampling rate, and augmentation strategy for video-based behavioral classification in data-scarce clinical domains.

</details>


### [227] [Agentic Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07984)
*Seokhoon Jeong,Mijung Kim,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentNAS，利用LLM生成种子架构并分解为"槽位架构"，自动定义NAS搜索空间，在17个多模态任务上取得SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦通用NAS方法（LLM+NAS协同），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测或轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对通用NAS方法有广泛调研需求，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用分类、密集回归、分割、多标签分类；场景：多模态通用任务（NAS-Bench-360和Unseen NAS）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。

Model architecture: AgentNAS三阶段流水线：LLM生成种子架构 → 分解为"槽位架构"（带命名可互换模块槽的脚手架）→ 传统NAS在自动定义的搜索空间内搜索。核心是LLM与NAS的分工协作机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文是NAS方法研究，但未讨论模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: NAS-Bench-360和Unseen NAS，涵盖17个任务（分类、密集回归、分割、多标签分类），评价指标为各任务的SOTA对比。

Experiments: 在17个任务中11个达到新SOTA，超越包括任务特定专家设计在内的基线。消融实验表明LLM种子和NAS搜索互补，该模式在三种不同能力LLM上均成立。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感场景，未在遥感数据集上验证；2. 未讨论模型轻量化或部署约束；3. 计算开销（LLM调用+NAS搜索）可能较大，不适合资源受限的星上场景；4. 未涉及目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 1. LLM+NAS协同设计架构的思路可借鉴，但需适配遥感目标检测的特定需求（如小目标、旋转框）；2. "槽位架构"概念可用于设计遥感专用轻量检测模型的模块化搜索空间；3. 可将LLM生成的架构作为轻量化检测模型的初始结构，再结合剪枝/量化进行压缩；4. 但整体而言，该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) methods have grown increasingly efficient, yet they remain bounded by manually engineered search spaces that require substantial domain expertise and must be rebuilt for every new task. Large language models (LLMs) can generate architectures in an open-ended space, but how to optimally divide the labor between LLM-driven design and NAS-driven search remains unexplored. We propose a mechanism that bridges these two paradigms: an LLM produces a high-quality seed architecture, then decomposes it into a "slotted architecture", a scaffold with named, interchangeable module slots that automatically defines a bounded, task-specific search space for conventional NAS to explore, without manual engineering. We instantiate this mechanism in AgentNAS, a modular three-phase pipeline in which each component's contribution can be measured independently. On 17 tasks spanning classification, dense regression, segmentation, and multi-label tagging across diverse modalities (NAS-Bench-360 and Unseen NAS), AgentNAS establishes a new state of the art on 11 tasks, outperforming published baselines including task-specific expert designs. Ablation studies show that the two search mechanisms are broadly complementary: the LLM-generated seed already surpasses published baselines on the majority of tasks, and NAS delivers additional gains in most cases through combinatorial recombination across slots, a mode of search that independent LLM samples cannot replicate. These patterns hold across three LLMs of different capability levels, confirming that the division of labor is robust. Our code is available at https://github.com/alroimfebruary/AgentNAS.

</details>


### [228] [Concretized Proposition Prompting Resolves Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08018)
*Changhun Lee,Minguk Jeon,Jongkyung Shin,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Concretized Proposition Prompting (CPP)框架，通过显式具体化与问题相关的命题，解决大语言模型中组合性与知识性之间的权衡问题，提升推理性能。
